HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000493/B002","root_000526/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_103a66cde3bb41a112b6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:21:1\",\"root_000526\",[\"B002\"]],[\"32:21:4\",\"root_000493\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yakınlık, inkâr edilen geleceğin sınırlı bir örneğini kullanılabilir bugünkü kanıta çevirir; değeri, tepkinin hâlâ sonucu değiştirebildiği zamanda gelmesidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000493/B001","root_000502/B001","root_001281/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2a37dfea8dc464cd4fc1","text":"1. **Near punishment before the greater threshold.** **د ن و B001-B002** supplies nearness and the nearer or first side; **د و ن B001** supplies what is near yet short of a limit; **ك ب ر B001-B002** supplies the greater scale and chief burden. The two punishments are ordered by distance and magnitude, and the purpose clause remains open. I infer that the nearer experience occurs on the still-reversible side of a threshold. الأدنى therefore reads temporally and functionally as an advance intervention, not only a smaller quantity of pain.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_30315eb0f02b0b5e4a3c","text":"3. **Proximity converts a denied future into usable present evidence.** The emphatic *la-nudhīqannahum* guarantees an experience, while *al-adnā* locates it before the greater endpoint. In 32:20, `ك ذ ب:B002` named denial and `ذ و ق:B002` named terminal experience; in 32:22, reminder will again precede a choice of orientation. **Inferred arrow:** a limited present contact with consequence -> interruption of denial -> renewed opportunity to face the reminder. The nearer punishment is meaningful precisely because it arrives while response remains causally effective.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3589586d0bbe09b7d3c3","text":"Later 32:22 showed why the purpose clause is only prospective, not guaranteed: after reminder, a person can still turn his side away. 32:26 then made destroyed generations another nearer warning, so the corrective tasting in 32:21 belongs to a wider sequence of interventions whose efficacy depends on response.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000526/B002","root_000544/B003","root_000994/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_410890cbac5573064ed4","text":"2. **Punishment as diagnostic testing.** **ذ و ق B002** supplies experiencing and testing; **ع ذ ب B005** supplies punishment; **ر ج ع B003** supplies turning back from an act or prior course. The explicit لَعَلَّهُمْ supplies the desired direction. I infer that this taste reveals the trajectory early enough to prompt reversal. The painful experience has a corrective function distinct from the closed recurrence of 32:20.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000493/B001","root_000493/B002","root_000502/B001","root_001281/B001","root_001281/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_52a108489242f536a586","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:21:4\",\"root_000493\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"32:21:5\",\"root_000502\",[\"B001\"]],[\"32:21:7\",\"root_001281\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yakın ve büyük azap, aynı şeyin iki adı değil, ilk tecrübenin büyük sonucun bu yanında kaldığı ve hâlâ ders verebildiği aşamalı bir ölçektir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000493/B002","root_000502/B001","root_001281/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5acb5d1fa3dfd2ef8f88","text":"1. **The two punishments form a scale rather than synonyms.** `د ن و:B002` directly covers *al-adnā* as the nearer, lower, smaller, or first side; `ك ب ر:B001` supplies greatness for *al-akbar*. The partitive *min* and `د و ن:B001`, below or short of a limit, place the first experience on this side of the greater one. **Packet relation:** some nearer punishment precedes and falls short of the greater punishment. **Inferred arrow:** differentiated scale -> an interval in which consequence can still instruct.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000526/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_87f4b8757f3c23907069","text":"2. **The stated purpose is corrective return.** `ذ و ق:B002` gives direct undergoing, and `ر ج ع:B003` specifically supplies turning back from sin or a prior course. *Laʿallahum yarjiʿūn* makes return the disclosed aim of the nearer tasting. **Packet arrow:** nearer experienced consequence -> possible turning back. This changes punishment from undifferentiated retribution into a diagnostic intervention whose success would be altered orientation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a93261bca79a887c5c91","text":"The reminder followed by turning away in 32:22 showed how the corrective opening can be refused. The final opening in 32:29, when faith no longer benefits and no respite is granted, later fixed the far boundary implied by **دُونَ** here.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000526/B002","root_000544/B003","root_000994/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c32cafa00449a6fad3ce","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"32:21:1\",\"root_000526\",[\"B002\"]],[\"32:21:3\",\"root_000994\",[\"B005\"]],[\"32:21:6\",\"root_000994\",[\"B005\"]],[\"32:21:9\",\"root_000544\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yakın azabın tattırılması, kişiye gittiği yönü sonucu kesinleşmeden deneyimletip önceki yolundan dönme imkânı veren düzeltici bir sınamadır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The nearer/greater syntax may dominate dūna's other-than branch and reduce the category distinction to a secondary resonance.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"broader_than_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:remaining_uncertainties/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_910c14119d5c652b705c","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
